# Codul de procedură civilă din 9 septembrie 1865

Codul de procedură civilă din 1865 · 29 versions, from 1865-12-01 (latest in force: 2013-02-15) · <https://legislatie.just.ro/Public/DetaliiDocument/2>

| Provision | File | Changes | Last changed |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |

Act id: `codul-procedura-civila-1865` (tags `codul-procedura-civila-1865/<day>`; `python tools/on.py <day> codul-procedura-civila-1865`).

## Decisions made by hand

- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocument/2 as captured on 2025-10-18, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 22 forms, 22 held.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the newest form held is that of 31.01.2013; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the first version is the code as published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 200 of 11 September 1865, put in force on 1 December 1865, as the portal's note on the republished text says ("decretat la 9 septembrie 1865, promulgat la 11 septembrie 1865 și pus în aplicare la 1 decembrie 1865"). Git cannot store a date before 1970: commits before then are dated 1 January 1970, a second apart, and their In-force trailer gives the real day.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the next version is the code republished in Monitorul Oficial nr. 45 of 24 February 1948 under art. 7 of Legea nr. 18/1948 (enacted and published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 35 of 12 February 1948; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/35558), which replaced Books I-III and renumbered the articles; it is dated 1 March 1948, the day that law took effect (its art. 8), and its changes are credited to Legea nr. 18/1948. Files follow each text's own numbering. The portal holds no form between 1865 and 1948, so the changes of those years arrive with the republished text.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the portal holds no form between the republication of 1948 and the form it lists under 23 July 1993, the date of Legea nr. 59/1993, whose changes that form prints; the law was published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 177 of 26 July 1993 and took effect that day (its art. 10; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/3195), so the form is read at 26 July 1993. The changes of 1948-1993 arrive with it, as the publisher's, save the repeals dated by their acts below. That form prints art. 612 alin. 4-6 under a title of their own ("Articolul 612^1"); they are read as part of art. 612, as Legea nr. 59/1993 and every later form give them. After art. 735 it prints articles of Legea nr. 18/1948 and Legea nr. 59/1993 and the portal's note introducing them; they are not this code's text.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: articles a form leaves out are dated by the act that repealed them, read as published: arts. 620-642 by art. 25 of Decretul nr. 40/1953 (Buletinul Oficial nr. 2 of 22 January 1953, in force on publication, its art. 28; DetaliiDocumentAfis/76); arts. 602-606, 677-691 and 698-709 by art. 49 of Decretul nr. 32/1954 (Buletinul Oficial nr. 32 of 31 January 1954; DetaliiDocumentAfis/83), on the day the Codul familiei took effect, 1 February 1954 (art. 161 of that code, as republished; DetaliiDocumentAfis/39247); arts. 643-673 and 692-697 by art. I pct. 221 and 223 of OUG nr. 138/2000, in force 2 May 2001 (its art. IX as OUG nr. 290/2000 rewrote it; DetaliiDocumentAfis/24428, 25814). Each is a version of its own on that day. The later forms that print them again as "Abrogate." are the publisher's (repeal stubs with no change in the law).
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865 (coordinator ruling): arts. 226-234, which the form of 1993 leaves out, were repealed, the later forms' note says, by "Decretul nr. 208 din 12 august 1950"; repealing act not held; dated by the date the portal's note gives for it (decrees of the period took effect on publication): a version of its own on 12 August 1950, credited to Decretul nr. 208/1950.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865 (the drop rules the coordinator gave for the Codul comercial din 1887, applied the same way): an article a form leaves out, or prints only as a range title with no text, that a later form prints again is carried through the forms in between with its earlier text. Arts. 710-720 are carried through the form of 2001 and shown "Abrogate." from 25 June 2003, with no credit: the later forms' note names Legea nr. 7/1996 and Legea nr. 99/1999, but read as published neither repeals them on a day (Legea nr. 7/1996 art. 72 ends their application county by county as the land registers are completed; Legea nr. 99/1999 title VI art. 104 leaves them out for movable securities). Arts. 108^1-108^5, 114^1, 274-277, 281^1-281^3, 308 and 329^1 are carried through the forms that leave them out with no note naming a repealing act.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the forms of 2001-2012 print, after the note on a repealed article or range, the portal's quotation of its old text ("În vechea reglementare, art. 643-673 aveau următorul conținut: "Art. 643. ...") over the following paragraphs, sometimes as a second article of the same number (art. 491, after art. 523) or inside the range (art. 472 in "Articolul 471-487"), or as bare "Art. 473" paragraphs after it; the quotation is the note's, not text. They print the next section's heading ("Secțiunea a II - a", "9. Asigurarea dovezilor") at the end of some articles; it is left out. The form of 16 November 2012 prints the heading of the chapter on costs as an article "Articolul IV" around arts. 274-277, which are read as the code's own.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the older forms print the amendment notes as footnotes ("*) Art. 612 a fost modificat prin art. 1 pct. 219 din Ordonanța de urgenta nr. 138/2000 publicată în M. Of. nr. 479 din 2 octombrie 2000"); they are read as notes, and an act named "nr. 138/2000 publicată în M. Of. nr. 479" is OUG nr. 138/2000 (likewise OUG nr. 59/2001, Monitorul Oficial nr. 217, and Legea nr. 59/1993, Monitorul Oficial nr. 177), not an act numbered after the gazette. Misprinted titles are read as the article they stand for: "Articolul 2O2" (1948) as art. 202, "Articolul 329^1-319^2" (2008) and "Articol" with "329^1 - 329^2" (2003) as the range 329^1-329^2; titles with a letter after the superscript ("Articolul 281^2a", "613^1a") keep the letter in the file name. A list numbered "I." to "VII." that ends an article after a line ending ":" (art. 659 of 1865) is its text, not the next articles' sub-heading.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: repealed in full by art. 83 lit. a) of Legea nr. 76/2012 pentru punerea în aplicare a Legii nr. 134/2010 privind Codul de procedură civilă (published in Monitorul Oficial nr. 365 of 30 May 2012; read as published, DetaliiDocumentAfis/138484), on the day the new Code of Civil Procedure took effect, 15 February 2013 (art. 81 alin. (1), 1 September 2012 as published, moved to 1 February 2013 by OUG nr. 44/2012 and to 15 February 2013 by OUG nr. 4/2013). The record ends with a version that removes every article, credited to Legea nr. 76/2012. The newest form the portal lists is that of 31 January 2013.
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 595 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «* Juramantul, ca mijloc de proba, nu mai exista în actuala r», «* Jurământul, ca mijloc de proba, nu mai exista în actuala r», «* Jurământul, ca mijloc de probă, nu mai există în actuala r».
- Codul de procedură civilă din 1865: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 226, left out from the form of 26.07.1993: repealed by Decretul nr. 208/1950, in force 12.08.1950 (repealing act not held; ## Constitutional Court findings not applied to this text

The Constitutional Court found the provisions below unconstitutional in a way the files here do not show as struck out: only some words inside a provision, a reading of it, a rule read from it, provisions read together with others, a provision amended or repealed before the decision took effect on it, a finding not matched automatically to one provision here, or a decision given before the 2003 revision of the Constitution. The text here is unchanged; read the decision. `data/court-notes.csv` lists every such finding.

- **[Decizia nr. 1/1995](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/1995/1/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 66 din 11 aprilie 1995 · art. 45 alin. (1) · decided before the 2003 revision of the Constitution

  > constata ca art. 45 alin. 1 din Codul de procedură civilă este neconstitutional în ce priveşte limitarea dreptului procurorului de a participa numai la procese privind "apărarea drepturilor şi intereselor legitime ale minorilor şi ale persoanelor puse sub interdicţia, precum şi în alte cazuri prevăzute de lege", urmînd ca în privinta acestei atribuţii să se aplice direct art. 130 alin. (1) din Constituţie.

  Given before the 2003 revision of the Constitution (no 45-day rule) and published on 1995-04-11. The decision was open to appeal (recurs) when given; a decision on appeal may have changed it. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 73/1996](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/1996/73/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 255 din 22 octombrie 1996 · art. 330^2 alin. (1) · decided before the 2003 revision of the Constitution

  > Admite excepţia de neconstituţionalitate a prevederilor art. 330^2 alin. 1 din Codul de procedură civilă \[…\]

  Given before the 2003 revision of the Constitution (no 45-day rule) and published on 1996-10-22. The decision was open to appeal (recurs) when given; a decision on appeal may have changed it. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 11/1999](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/1999/11/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 136 din 1 aprilie 1999 · art. 22 alin. (5) · decided before the 2003 revision of the Constitution

  > constata ca dispoziţia art. 22 alin. 5 din Codul de procedură civilă, conform căreia termenul pentru exercitarea dreptului de recurs curge de la pronunţarea hotărârii care conţine regulatorul de competenţa, este neconstitutionala.

  Given before the 2003 revision of the Constitution (no 45-day rule) and published on 1999-04-01; final. Under Constituția din 1991, art. 145 alin. (2), the Court's decisions are binding and have effect only for the future. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 194/2004](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2004/194/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 523 din 10 iunie 2004 · art. 308 alin. (4) · only some words or a sentence inside the provision

  > Admite excepţia de neconstituţionalitate a dispoziţiilor art. 308 alin. 4 din Codul de procedură civilă, modificate şi completate prin Ordonanţa de urgenţă a Guvernului nr. 58/2003 \[…\] şi constată că acest text de lege este neconstituţional în partea exprimată prin sintagma "pronunţată fără citarea părţilor".

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2004-06-10) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2004-07-25, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 737/2008](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2008/737/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 562 din 25 iulie 2008 · art. 302 · only part of a provision (a point, indent, note or annex, or a narrower finding)

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 302 din Codul de procedură civilă sunt neconstituţionale în partea care prevede "sub sancţiunea nulităţii".

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2008-07-25) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2008-09-08, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 303/2009](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2009/303/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 239 din 10 aprilie 2009 · art. 288 alin. (2) · only some words or a sentence inside the provision

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 288 alin. 2 teza finală, "sub sancţiunea nulităţii", din Codul de procedură civilă sunt neconstituţionale.

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2009-04-10) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2009-05-25, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 233/2011](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2011/233/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 340 din 17 mai 2011 · art. 322 · constitutional only as the Court reads it (an interpretive decision)

  > constată că dispoziţiile art. 322 pct. 9 din Codul de procedură civilă sunt neconstituţionale în măsura în care nu permit revizuirea unei hotărâri judecătoreşti prin care, fără a se evoca fondul, s-au produs încălcări ale unor drepturi şi libertăţi fundamentale, încălcări constatate de Curtea Europeană a Drepturilor Omului.

  The Court admitted the exception and found the provision constitutional only as it reads it in the decision (or unconstitutional only in the reading it names); the words stay. The decision is generally binding from its publication (2011-05-17) (Constituția art. 147 alin. (4)). The text here is unchanged; read the decision.

- **[Decizia nr. 967/2012](../../../jurisprudenta/ccr/2012/967/)**, Monitorul Oficial nr. 853 din 18 decembrie 2012 · art. 1; art. 299 alin. (1^1) · only part of a provision (a point, indent, note or annex, or a narrower finding)

  > constată că prevederile art. 1 pct. 1^1 şi art. 299 alin. 1^1 din Codul de procedură civilă sunt neconstituţionale.

  Under Constituția art. 147 alin. (1), what the decision found unconstitutional is suspended from its publication (2012-12-18) and ceases to have legal effect 45 days later, on 2013-02-01, unless Parliament or the Government brings it into line with the Constitution in that time. The text here is unchanged; read the decision.
